# Skeptical Condition Search Audit

## Boundary

- This run challenges ACTIONABLE-only, extended exclusion, clear-geometry rejection, geometry-caution filtering, freshness requirements, below-buy-point rejection, entry-volume requirements, volume-confirmation requirements, industry cover, ordering, EPS gate, and max recommendation slots.
- Selectors still use only pool fields and deterministic ranks. Realized returns are used only after replay for evaluation and finalist selection.
- All generated schemes are evaluated weekly/proxy. Backtrader is run on all 2595 variants: generated skeptical configs plus baselines.

## Conclusion

- No robust replacement beat the baseline across Backtrader return, drawdown, stops, weekly median, weekly floor, Bottom5 exposure, pick stop rate, and abstain quality.
- Baseline `skill_industry_eps_known`: final 15470.50, return 54.71%, max DD -16.90%, stops 5.
- Best Backtrader scheme `sk_sig_epspass_coreentryloose_freshstrict_bpallow_geomclean_noind_prox_top3`: final 21541.85, return 115.42%, max DD -5.45%, stops 1, status portfolio_tradeoff.
- non-ACTIONABLE entered the experiment: best Backtrader non-ACTIONABLE scheme `sk_sigext_epspass_corestrict_freshstrict_bpstrict_geomallow_noind_prox_top2` final 17835.42, return 78.35%, max DD -10.41%, stops 2, pick rate 10.71%, status portfolio_tradeoff.
- extended entered the experiment: best Backtrader extended scheme `sk_sigext_epspass_corestrict_freshstrict_bpstrict_geomallow_noind_prox_top2` final 17835.42, return 78.35%, max DD -10.41%, stops 2, pick rate 10.71%, status portfolio_tradeoff.
- clear-geometry-failure entered the experiment: best Backtrader clear-geometry-failure scheme `sk_sigext_epspass_corestrict_freshstrict_bpstrict_geomallow_noind_prox_top2` final 17835.42, return 78.35%, max DD -10.41%, stops 2, pick rate 8.33%, status portfolio_tradeoff.
- freshness-missing was allowed in the search, but no Backtradered top picks used it.
- below-candidate-buy-point was allowed in the search, but no Backtradered top picks used it.
- Proxy-only note: best non-ACTIONABLE proxy scheme `sk_sig_epsknown_coreloose_freshallow_bpstrict_geomallow_ind_def_top3` reached proxy final 120072455.12, but replacement decisions above use Backtrader only.
